Together, these findings support massive option evaluation as a general framework for stress testing model reliability under extreme distractor density, beyond what low option benchmarks can reveal.

</details>


### [113] [Fact4ac at the Financial Misinformation Detection Challenge Task: Reference-Free Financial Misinformation Detection via Fine-Tuning and Few-Shot Prompting of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14640)
*Cuong Hoang,Le-Minh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于大语言模型的参考无关金融虚假信息检测方法，在RFC-BENCH基准测试中取得了第一名，公开测试集准确率达95.4%，私有测试集达96.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融虚假信息的泛滥严重威胁市场稳定和投资者信任，导致市场行为误导和信息不对称。在无法获取外部证据或参考信息进行交叉验证的现实场景中，检测误导性叙述具有挑战性。

Method: 提出一个综合框架，利用最先进大语言模型的推理能力，系统整合上下文学习（零样本和少样本提示策略）与参数高效微调（通过LoRA低秩适应），使模型能够捕捉金融操纵的微妙语言线索。

Result: 在"Reference-Free Financial Misinformation Detection"共享任务的两个官方排行榜上均获得第一名，公开测试集准确率95.4%，私有测试集准确率96.3%，证明了方法的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该方法通过结合上下文学习和参数高效微调，有效提升了金融自然语言处理中上下文感知的虚假信息检测能力，加速了该领域的发展，模型已开源供研究使用。

Abstract: The proliferation of financial misinformation poses a severe threat to market stability and investor trust, misleading market behavior and creating critical information asymmetry. Detecting such misleading narratives is inherently challenging, particularly in real-world scenarios where external evidence or supplementary references for cross-verification are strictly unavailable. This paper presents our winning methodology for the "Reference-Free Financial Misinformation Detection" shared task. Built upon the recently proposed RFC-BENCH framework (Jiang et al. 2026), this task challenges models to determine the veracity of financial claims by relying solely on internal semantic understanding and contextual consistency, rather than external fact-checking. To address this formidable evaluation setup, we propose a comprehensive framework that capitalizes on the reasoning capabilities of state-of-the-art Large Language Models (LLMs). Our approach systematically integrates in-context learning, specifically zero-shot and few-shot prompting strategies, with Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) via Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) to optimally align the models with the subtle linguistic cues of financial manipulation. Our proposed system demonstrated superior efficacy, successfully securing the first-place ranking on both official leaderboards. Specifically, we achieved an accuracy of 95.4% on the public test set and 96.3% on the private test set, highlighting the robustness of our method and contributing to the acceleration of context-aware misinformation detection in financial Natural Language Processing. Our models (14B and 32B) are available at https://huggingface.co/KaiNKaiho.

</details>


### [114] [CURaTE: Continual Unlearning in Real Time with Ensured Preservation of LLM Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14644)
*Seyun Bae,Seokhan Lee,Eunho Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CURaTE是一种实时持续遗忘方法，通过训练句子嵌入模型建立决策边界，判断输入是否与遗忘请求相关，从而决定回答或拒绝，无需修改LLM参数即可实现高效遗忘和知识保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遗忘方法无法在预训练前完全过滤问题数据，且缺乏持续实时处理能力，导致累积更新时效用下降和敏感信息长期暴露。

Method: 训练句子嵌入模型在特定数据集上形成锐利决策边界，用于判断输入提示是否与存储的遗忘请求相关，根据相似度决定回答或拒绝响应，不修改语言模型参数。

Result: CURaTE比现有方法实现更有效的遗忘，通过避免修改模型参数，在任意数量更新中保持近乎完美的知识保留，是唯一能实时持续遗忘的方法。

Conclusion: CURaTE通过简单但有效的方法解决了LLM持续实时遗忘的挑战，在遗忘效果和知识保留方面均优于现有技术，为实际应用提供了可行解决方案。

Abstract: The inability to filter out in advance all potentially problematic data from the pre-training of large language models has given rise to the need for methods for unlearning specific pieces of knowledge after training. Existing techniques overlook the need for continuous and immediate action, causing them to suffer from degraded utility as updates accumulate and protracted exposure of sensitive information. To address these issues, we propose Continual Unlearning in Real Time with Ensured Preservation of LLM Knowledge (CURaTE). Our method begins by training a sentence embedding model on a dataset designed to enable the formation of sharp decision boundaries for determining whether a given input prompt corresponds to any stored forget requests. The similarity of a given input to the forget requests is then used to determine whether to answer or return a refusal response. We show that even with such a simple approach, not only does CURaTE achieve more effective forgetting than existing methods, but by avoiding modification of the language model parameters, it also maintains near perfect knowledge preservation over any number of updates and is the only method capable of continual unlearning in real-time.

</details>


### [115] [CURA: Clinical Uncertainty Risk Alignment for Language Model-Based Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14651)
*Sizhe Wang,Ziqi Xu,Claire Najjuuko,Charles Alba,Chenyang Lu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CURA框架通过个体级校准和群体感知正则化，提升临床语言模型风险预测的不确定性校准和临床可靠性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床语言模型在自由文本笔记的风险预测中应用日益广泛，但其不确定性估计通常校准不佳且临床可靠性不足，需要改进不确定性校准方法

Method: 提出CURA框架：1) 微调领域特定临床LM获取任务适应患者嵌入；2) 使用双层不确定性目标对多头分类器进行不确定性微调，包括个体级校准项（对齐预测不确定性与患者错误可能性）和群体感知正则化（将风险估计拉向嵌入空间局部邻域的事件率，并在决策边界附近的模糊群体上施加额外权重）

Result: 在MIMIC-IV临床风险预测任务上的广泛实验表明，CURA能一致改善校准指标而不显著损害判别能力，减少过度自信的虚假保证，为下游临床决策支持提供更可信的不确定性估计

Conclusion: CURA框架通过同时对齐个体级和群体级不确定性，有效提升了临床语言模型风险预测的校准性和临床可靠性，群体感知正则化可解释为具有邻域信息软标签的交叉熵损失

Abstract: Clinical language models (LMs) are increasingly applied to support clinical risk prediction from free-text notes, yet their uncertainty estimates often remain poorly calibrated and clinically unreliable. In this work, we propose Clinical Uncertainty Risk Alignment (CURA), a framework that aligns clinical LM-based risk estimates and uncertainty with both individual error likelihoods and cohort-level ambiguities. CURA first fine-tunes domain-specific clinical LMs to obtain task-adapted patient embeddings, and then performs uncertainty fine-tuning of a multi-head classifier using a bi-level uncertainty objective. Specifically, an individual-level calibration term aligns predictive uncertainty with each patient's likelihood of error, while a cohort-aware regularizer pulls risk estimates toward event rates in their local neighborhoods in the embedding space and places extra weight on ambiguous cohorts near the decision boundary. We further show that this cohort-aware term can be interpreted as a cross-entropy loss with neighborhood-informed soft labels, providing a label-smoothing view of our method. Extensive experiments on MIMIC-IV clinical risk prediction tasks across various clinical LMs show that CURA consistently improves calibration metrics without substantially compromising discrimination. Further analysis illustrates that CURA reduces overconfident false reassurance and yields more trustworthy uncertainty estimates for downstream clinical decision support.

</details>


### [116] [SPAGBias: Uncovering and Tracing Structured Spatial Gender Bias in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14672)
*Binxian Su,Haoye Lou,Shucheng Zhu,Weikang Wang,Ying Liu,Dong Yu,Pengyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SPAGBias是首个评估LLM空间性别偏见的系统框架，通过62种城市微空间分类、提示库和三层诊断方法，发现LLM存在超越公私领域划分的结构化性别-空间关联，这些偏见在模型全流程中嵌入并强化，导致实际应用失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在城市规划中的广泛应用，基于性别空间理论（强调性别等级如何嵌入空间组织），研究者担心LLM可能复制或放大此类偏见。目前缺乏系统评估LLM空间性别偏见的框架，需要连接社会学理论与计算分析，将偏见研究扩展到空间领域。

Method: 提出SPAGBias框架：1) 62种城市微空间分类法；2) 提示库；3) 三层诊断：显性（强制选择重采样）、概率（词元级不对称性）、建构性（语义和叙事角色分析）。测试六个代表性模型，分析提示设计、温度和模型规模对偏见表达的影响，并通过追踪实验考察偏见在预训练、指令微调和奖励建模全流程中的嵌入机制。

Result: 发现LLM存在超越传统公私领域划分的结构化性别-空间关联，形成细粒度的微观映射。故事生成揭示情感、措辞和社会角色共同塑造"空间性别叙事"。模型关联显著超过真实世界分布，下游实验显示这些偏见在规范性和描述性应用场景中均导致具体失败。

Conclusion: 该工作将社会学理论与计算分析相结合，将偏见研究扩展到空间领域，揭示了LLM如何通过语言编码社会性别认知。SPAGBias为系统评估和缓解LLM的空间性别偏见提供了首个框架，对负责任的城市规划AI应用具有重要意义。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are being increasingly used in urban planning, but since gendered space theory highlights how gender hierarchies are embedded in spatial organization, there is concern that LLMs may reproduce or amplify such biases. We introduce SPAGBias - the first systematic framework to evaluate spatial gender bias in LLMs. It combines a taxonomy of 62 urban micro-spaces, a prompt library, and three diagnostic layers: explicit (forced-choice resampling), probabilistic (token-level asymmetry), and constructional (semantic and narrative role analysis). Testing six representative models, we identify structured gender-space associations that go beyond the public-private divide, forming nuanced micro-level mappings. Story generation reveals how emotion, wording, and social roles jointly shape "spatial gender narratives". We also examine how prompt design, temperature, and model scale influence bias expression. Tracing experiments indicate that these patterns are embedded and reinforced across the model pipeline (pre-training, instruction tuning, and reward modeling), with model associations found to substantially exceed real-world distributions. Downstream experiments further reveal that such biases produce concrete failures in both normative and descriptive application settings. This work connects sociological theory with computational analysis, extending bias research into the spatial domain and uncovering how LLMs encode social gender cognition through language.

</details>


### [117] [Which bird does not have wings: Negative-constrained KGQA with Schema-guided Semantic Matching and Self-directed Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14749)
*Midan Shim,Seokju Hwang,Kaehyun Um,Kyong-Ho Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出NEST-KGQA新任务和数据集，针对知识图谱问答中负约束被忽视的问题，开发PyLF逻辑形式和CUCKOO框架来处理多约束复杂语义问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识图谱问答基准和方法偏向正约束和计算约束，忽视了实际中常见的负约束问题，需要专门处理负约束和多约束的解决方案

Method: 1) 提出NEST-KGQA任务和数据集；2) 设计Python格式的PyLF逻辑形式清晰表达否定；3) 开发CUCKOO框架：首先生成约束感知逻辑形式草稿，进行模式引导的语义匹配，然后选择性应用自导向精炼

Result: CUCKOO在少样本设置下，在传统KGQA和NEST-KGQA基准测试中均持续优于基线方法

Conclusion: 论文成功解决了KGQA中负约束被忽视的问题，提出的NEST-KGQA任务、PyLF逻辑形式和CUCKOO框架有效处理了多约束复杂语义问题，提升了问答系统的鲁棒性和准确性

Abstract: Large language models still struggle with faithfulness and hallucinations despite their remarkable reasoning abilities. In Knowledge Graph Question Answering (KGQA), semantic parsing-based approaches address the limitations by understanding constraints in a user's question and converting them into a logical form to execute on a knowledge graph. However, existing KGQA benchmarks and methods are biased toward positive and calculation constraints. Negative constraints are neglected, although they frequently appear in real-world questions. In this paper, we introduce a new task, NEgative-conSTrained (NEST) KGQA, where each question contains at least one negative constraint, and a corresponding dataset, NestKGQA. We also design PyLF, a Python-formatted logical form, since existing logical forms are hardly suitable to express negation clearly while maintaining readability. Furthermore, NEST questions naturally contain multiple constraints. To mitigate their semantic complexity, we present a novel framework named CUCKOO, specialized to multiple-constrained questions and ensuring semantic executability. CUCKOO first generates a constraint-aware logical form draft and performs schema-guided semantic matching. It then selectively applies self-directed refinement only when executing improper logical forms yields an empty result, reducing cost while improving robustness. Experimental results demonstrate that CUCKOO consistently outperforms baselines on both conventional KGQA and NEST-KGQA benchmarks under few-shot settings.

</details>


### [118] [CoPA: Benchmarking Personalized Question Answering with Data-Informed Cognitive Factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14773)
*Hang Su,Zequn Liu,Chen Hu,Xuesong Lu,Yingce Xia,Zhen Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CoPA：基于社区-个体偏好分歧挖掘的个性化问答评估基准，包含1,985个用户画像，提供细粒度因子级评估标准


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在问答中的个性化评估主要依赖词汇级相似度或手动启发式方法，缺乏足够的数据驱动验证，需要更全面的评估标准

Method: 通过挖掘社区-个体偏好分歧（CIPD），即个体选择超越共识的情况，提炼出六个关键个性化因子作为评估维度，构建CoPA基准

Result: 创建了包含1,985个用户画像的CoPA基准，通过量化模型输出与用户特定认知偏好的对齐程度，提供比通用指标更全面和区分性的个性化QA评估标准

Conclusion: CoPA通过基于CIPD的因子级评估，为个性化问答提供了更全面、更具区分性的评估框架，填补了现有评估方法的不足

Abstract: While LLMs have demonstrated remarkable potential in Question Answering (QA), evaluating personalization remains a critical bottleneck. Existing paradigms predominantly rely on lexical-level similarity or manual heuristics, often lacking sufficient data-driven validation. We address this by mining Community-Individual Preference Divergence (CIPD), where individual choices override consensus, to distill six key personalization factors as evaluative dimensions. Accordingly, we introduce CoPA, a benchmark with 1,985 user profiles for fine-grained, factor-level assessment. By quantifying the alignment between model outputs and user-specific cognitive preferences inferred from interaction patterns, CoPA provides a more comprehensive and discriminative standard for evaluating personalized QA than generic metrics. The code is available at https://github.com/bjzgcai/CoPA.

</details>


### [119] [Knowing When Not to Answer: Evaluating Abstention in Multimodal Reasoning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14799)
*Nishanth Madhusudhan,Vikas Yadav,Alexandre Lacoste*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MM-AQA基准测试评估多模态系统的有效弃权能力，发现现有模型在证据不足时很少弃权，多智能体系统能改善但存在准确率-弃权权衡，需要弃权感知训练而非更好的提示或更多智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型和多智能体系统的评估范式假设所有问题都可回答，迫使模型总是回应。弃权在纯文本环境中已有研究，但在多模态场景中探索不足，当前基准要么忽略不可回答性，要么依赖粗糙方法而错过真实的失败模式。

Method: 引入MM-AQA基准，通过两个维度（视觉模态依赖性和证据充分性）的变换，从可回答实例构建不可回答实例。评估了三个前沿视觉语言模型（涵盖闭源和开源）和两种多智能体架构，共2079个样本。

Result: (1) 标准提示下，视觉语言模型很少弃权；简单的置信度基线优于此设置；(2) 多智能体系统改善弃权但引入准确率-弃权权衡；(3) 顺序设计匹配或超过迭代变体，表明瓶颈是校准错误而非推理深度；(4) 当图像或文本证据缺失时模型会弃权，但会尝试协调退化或矛盾证据。

Conclusion: 有效的多模态弃权需要弃权感知训练，而非更好的提示或更多智能体。当前模型在证据不足时缺乏弃权能力，这是多模态系统可靠性的关键挑战。

Abstract: Effective abstention (EA), recognizing evidence insufficiency and refraining from answering, is critical for reliable multimodal systems. Yet existing evaluation paradigms for vision-language models (VLMs) and multi-agent systems (MAS) assume answerability, pushing models to always respond. Abstention has been studied in text-only settings but remains underexplored multimodally; current benchmarks either ignore unanswerability or rely on coarse methods that miss realistic failure modes. We introduce MM-AQA, a benchmark that constructs unanswerable instances from answerable ones via transformations along two axes: visual modality dependency and evidence sufficiency. Evaluating three frontier VLMs spanning closed and open-source models and two MAS architectures across 2079 samples, we find: (1) under standard prompting, VLMs rarely abstain; even simple confidence baselines outperform this setup, (2) MAS improves abstention but introduces an accuracy-abstention trade-off, (3) sequential designs match or exceed iterative variants, suggesting the bottleneck is miscalibration rather than reasoning depth, and (4) models abstain when image or text evidence is absent, but attempt reconciliation with degraded or contradictory evidence. Effective multimodal abstention requires abstention-aware training rather than better prompting or more agents.

</details>


### [120] [Modeling LLM Unlearning as an Asymmetric Two-Task Learning Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14808)
*Zeguan Xiao,Siqing Li,Yong Wang,Xuetao Wei,Jian Yang,Yun Chen,Guanhua Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种保留优先的梯度合成框架，将LLM遗忘重构为不对称双任务问题，通过解耦任务特定梯度提取和冲突感知组合来缓解遗忘-保留权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型遗忘旨在移除特定知识同时保持通用能力，但现有方法面临遗忘与保留之间的权衡问题。作者认为重新平衡损失并非最佳解决方案，而应关注梯度几何形状的优化。

Method: 提出保留优先的梯度合成框架，将LLM遗忘重构为不对称双任务问题（保留为主目标，遗忘为辅助任务）。框架包含任务特定梯度提取和冲突感知组合两个阶段。具体实现包括：1）采用PCGrad解决梯度冲突；2）提出SAGO方法，通过构造性符号约束合成实现更紧密的对齐。

Result: 在WMDP Bio/Cyber和RWKU基准测试中，SAGO持续推动帕累托前沿。例如在WMDP Bio（SimNPO+GD）上，目标模型MMLU性能恢复率从44.6%（朴素方法）提升至94.0%（+PCGrad），进一步达到96.0%（+SAGO），同时保持相当的遗忘强度。

Conclusion: 研究表明，重塑梯度几何形状而非重新平衡损失是缓解遗忘-保留权衡的关键。保留优先的梯度合成框架为LLM遗忘提供了有效解决方案，SAGO方法通过构造性符号约束合成实现了更优的性能。

Abstract: Machine unlearning for large language models (LLMs) aims to remove targeted knowledge while preserving general capability. In this paper, we recast LLM unlearning as an asymmetric two-task problem: retention is the primary objective and forgetting is an auxiliary. From this perspective, we propose a retention-prioritized gradient synthesis framework that decouples task-specific gradient extraction from conflict-aware combination. Instantiating the framework, we adapt established PCGrad to resolve gradient conflicts, and introduce SAGO, a novel retention-prioritized gradient synthesis method. Theoretically, both variants ensure non-negative cosine similarity with the retain gradient, while SAGO achieves strictly tighter alignment through constructive sign-constrained synthesis. Empirically, on WMDP Bio/Cyber and RWKU benchmarks, SAGO consistently pushes the Pareto frontier: e.g., on WMDP Bio (SimNPO+GD), recovery of target model MMLU performance progresses from 44.6% (naive) to 94.0% (+PCGrad) and further to 96.0% (+SAGO), while maintaining comparable forgetting strength. Our results show that re-shaping gradient geometry, rather than re-balancing losses, is the key to mitigating unlearning-retention trade-offs.

</details>


### [121] [Domain Fine-Tuning FinBERT on Finnish Histopathological Reports: Train-Time Signals and Downstream Correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14815)
*Rami Luisto,Liisa Petäinen,Tommi Grönholm,Jan Böhm,Maarit Ahtiainen,Tomi Lilja,Ilkka Pölönen,Sami Äyrämö*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究探索了在芬兰语医疗文本上对芬兰语BERT模型进行领域微调的效果，并尝试通过观察嵌入几何变化来预测领域特定预训练的收益


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在医疗AI领域，获取标注数据通常存在长时间延迟，特别是在标签获取方面。研究旨在探索在标注数据有限的情况下，通过领域特定微调来提升NLP分类任务性能的方法

Method: 1) 在芬兰语医疗文本数据上对芬兰语BERT模型进行领域微调；2) 通过观察领域微调引起的嵌入几何变化，尝试预测领域特定预训练的收益

Result: 论文报告了芬兰语BERT在医疗文本上微调的观察结果，以及通过嵌入几何分析预测领域预训练收益的尝试

Conclusion: 该研究为医疗AI领域在标注数据稀缺情况下提供了有效的模型优化方法，通过嵌入几何分析为预测领域预训练收益提供了新思路

Abstract: In NLP classification tasks where little labeled data exists, domain fine-tuning of transformer models on unlabeled data is an established approach. In this paper we have two aims.
  (1) We describe our observations from fine-tuning the Finnish BERT model on Finnish medical text data.
  (2) We report on our attempts to predict the benefit of domain-specific pre-training of Finnish BERT from observing the geometry of embedding changes due to domain fine-tuning.
  Our driving motivation is the common\situation in healthcare AI where we might experience long delays in acquiring datasets, especially with respect to labels.

</details>


### [122] [Exploring and Testing Skill-Based Behavioral Profile Annotation: Human Operability and LLM Feasibility under Schema-Guided Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14843)
*Yufeng Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究将行为特征标注视为多项技能的组合而非单一任务，通过技能文件驱动流程评估LLM辅助标注，发现标注技能高度异质，GPT-5.4在保留技能上表现可靠但选择性可行，开源模型存在模式执行问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为特征标注难以自动化，因为它需要跨多个语言学维度同时编码。研究将BP标注视为一组标注技能而非单一任务，从技能层面评估LLM辅助标注的可行性。

Method: 使用3,134个中文隐喻颜色词衍生词的共现行和14特征BP模式，实施技能文件驱动流程，每个特征通过模式文件、决策规则和示例外部定义。两名人类标注者完成两轮仅模式协议，将BP技能分类为可直接操作、可恢复或结构未指定。在相同设置下评估GPT-5.4和三个本地可部署开源模型。

Result: BP标注在技能层面高度异质：5项技能可直接操作，4项可通过重点重新标注恢复，5项保持结构未指定。GPT-5.4执行保留技能具有实质性可靠性（准确率=0.678，κ=0.665，加权F1=0.695），但可行性是选择性而非全局的。人类和GPT难度分布在技能层面高度一致（r=0.881），但在实例层面（r=0.016）和词汇项层面（r=-0.142）不一致。开源模型的失败集中在模式到技能执行问题。

Conclusion: 自动标注应在技能可行性而非任务层面自动化方面进行评估。GPT应被理解为独立的第三技能声音而非直接的人类替代品。研究支持技能驱动的标注评估框架。

Abstract: Behavioral Profile (BP) annotation is difficult to automate because it requires simultaneous coding across multiple linguistic dimensions. We treat BP annotation as a bundle of annotation skills rather than a single task and evaluate LLM-assisted BP annotation from this perspective. Using 3,134 concordance lines of 30 Chinese metaphorical color-term derivatives and a 14-feature BP schema, we implement a skill-file-driven pipeline in which each feature is externally defined through schema files, decision rules, and examples. Two human annotators completed a two-round schema-only protocol on a 300-instance validation subset, enabling BP skills to be classified as directly operable, recoverable under focused re-annotation, or structurally underspecified. GPT-5.4 and three locally deployable open-source models were then evaluated under the same setup. Results show that BP annotation is highly heterogeneous at the skill level: 5 skills are directly operable, 4 are recoverable after focused re-annotation, and 5 remain structurally underspecified. GPT-5.4 executes the retained skills with substantial reliability (accuracy = 0.678, \k{appa} = 0.665, weighted F1 = 0.695), but this feasibility is selective rather than global. Human and GPT difficulty profiles are strongly aligned at the skill level (r = 0.881), but not at the instance level (r = 0.016) or lexical-item level (r = -0.142), a pattern we describe as shared taxonomy, independent execution. Pairwise agreement further suggests that GPT is better understood as an independent third skill voice than as a direct human substitute. Open-source failures are concentrated in schema-to-skill execution problems. These findings suggest that automatic annotation should be evaluated in terms of skill feasibility rather than task-level automation.

</details>


### [123] [Segment-Level Coherence for Robust Harmful Intent Probing in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14865)
*Xuanli He,Bilgehan Sel,Faizan Ali,Jenny Bao,Hoagy Cunningham,Jerry Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种流式探测方法，通过要求多个证据令牌一致支持预测而非依赖孤立的高分令牌，显著提升CBRN领域有害内容检测的鲁棒性和准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在CBRN等高风险领域面临自适应越狱攻击，现有流式探测方法依赖少数高分令牌，导致在良性上下文中出现敏感CBRN术语时产生误报。

Method: 引入流式探测目标函数，要求多个证据令牌一致支持预测而非依赖孤立峰值，基于聚合信号而非单令牌线索进行更鲁棒的检测。

Result: 在1%固定误报率下，相比强流式基线方法，真阳性率相对提升35.55%；AUROC显著改善，即使基线性能接近饱和(AUROC=97.40%)；注意力或MLP激活的探测始终优于残差流特征；即使对抗微调启用新型字符级密码，有害意图仍可检测。

Conclusion: 提出的多证据令牌聚合方法显著提升了CBRN领域有害内容检测的鲁棒性和准确性，即使面对新型字符级密码等高级越狱技术，基于基础LLM开发的探测器仍能以"即插即用"方式有效工作。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly exposed to adaptive jailbreaking, particularly in high-stakes Chemical, Biological, Radiological, and Nuclear (CBRN) domains. Although streaming probes enable real-time monitoring, they still make systematic errors. We identify a core issue: existing methods often rely on a few high-scoring tokens, leading to false alarms when sensitive CBRN terms appear in benign contexts. To address this, we introduce a streaming probing objective that requires multiple evidence tokens to consistently support a prediction, rather than relying on isolated spikes. This encourages more robust detection based on aggregated signals instead of single-token cues. At a fixed 1% false-positive rate, our method improves the true-positive rate by 35.55% relative to strong streaming baselines. We further observe substantial gains in AUROC, even when starting from near-saturated baseline performance (AUROC = 97.40%). We also show that probing Attention or MLP activations consistently outperforms residual-stream features. Finally, even when adversarial fine-tuning enables novel character-level ciphers, harmful intent remains detectable: probes developed for the base LLMs can be applied ``plug-and-play'' to these obfuscated attacks, achieving an AUROC of over 98.85%.

</details>


### [124] [Reasoning Dynamics and the Limits of Monitoring Modality Reliance in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14888)
*Danae Sánchez Villegas,Samuel Lewis-Lim,Nikolaos Aletras,Desmond Elliott*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究分析了18个视觉语言模型(VLMs)的推理动态，发现模型存在"答案惯性"问题，早期预测倾向被强化而非修正。虽然推理训练模型表现出更强的纠正行为，但增益受模态条件影响。模型易受误导性文本线索影响，即使视觉证据足够，且思维链(CoT)仅能部分揭示模态依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管视觉语言模型展现出推理能力，但其推理过程如何整合视觉和文本信息仍不清楚。研究者希望了解VLMs的推理动态，特别是思维链(CoT)在多大程度上能揭示模型决策背后的模态依赖，这对多模态系统的透明度和安全性有重要意义。

Method: 研究分析了18个VLMs，涵盖指令微调和推理训练模型，来自两个不同的模型家族。通过跟踪CoT过程中的置信度变化，测量推理的纠正效果，评估中间推理步骤的贡献。使用带有误导性文本线索的受控干预，分析模型对文本线索的敏感度，并评估CoT是否能检测到这种影响。

Result: 发现模型存在"答案惯性"现象，早期预测在推理步骤中被强化而非修正。推理训练模型表现出更强的纠正行为，但增益受模态条件影响。模型普遍易受误导性文本线索影响，即使视觉证据足够。CoT仅能部分揭示模态依赖：推理训练模型更可能明确提及线索，但其流畅的CoT可能掩盖实际对文本的依赖；指令微调模型较少明确提及线索，但其简短的推理痕迹能更直接地揭示与视觉输入的不一致。

Conclusion: 思维链(CoT)仅提供了关于不同模态如何驱动VLM决策的部分视图，这对多模态系统的透明度和安全性有重要影响。需要更全面的方法来理解VLMs的推理动态和模态依赖。

Abstract: Recent advances in vision language models (VLMs) offer reasoning capabilities, yet how these unfold and integrate visual and textual information remains unclear. We analyze reasoning dynamics in 18 VLMs covering instruction-tuned and reasoning-trained models from two different model families. We track confidence over Chain-of-Thought (CoT), measure the corrective effect of reasoning, and evaluate the contribution of intermediate reasoning steps. We find that models are prone to answer inertia, in which early commitments to a prediction are reinforced, rather than revised during reasoning steps. While reasoning-trained models show stronger corrective behavior, their gains depend on modality conditions, from text-dominant to vision-only settings. Using controlled interventions with misleading textual cues, we show that models are consistently influenced by these cues even when visual evidence is sufficient, and assess whether this influence is recoverable from CoT. Although this influence can appear in the CoT, its detectability varies across models and depends on what is being monitored. Reasoning-trained models are more likely to explicitly refer to the cues, but their longer and fluent CoTs can still appear visually grounded while actually following textual cues, obscuring modality reliance. In contrast, instruction-tuned models refer to the cues less explicitly, but their shorter traces reveal inconsistencies with the visual input. Taken together, these findings indicate that CoT provides only a partial view of how different modalities drive VLM decisions, with important implications for the transparency and safety of multimodal systems.

</details>


### [125] [Comparison of Modern Multilingual Text Embedding Techniques for Hate Speech Detection Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14907)
*Evaldas Vaiciukynas,Paulius Danenas,Linas Ablonskis,Algirdas Sukys,Edgaras Dambrauskas,Voldemaras Zitkus,Rita Butkiene,Rimantas Butleris*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究评估多语言句子嵌入模型在立陶宛语、俄语和英语仇恨言论检测中的性能，引入LtHate新语料库，比较六种嵌入模型和两种分类方法，发现监督分类显著优于异常检测，PCA压缩在监督场景中保持性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线仇恨言论和辱骂性语言在多语言环境特别是低资源语言（如立陶宛语）中构成日益严峻的内容审核挑战，需要评估现代多语言句子嵌入模型在这些语言中的仇恨言论检测能力。

Method: 引入新的立陶宛语仇恨言论语料库LtHate，在LtHate、RuToxic和EnSuperset三个数据集上基准测试六种多语言编码器（potion、gemma、bge、snow、jina、e5）。对每种嵌入训练单类HBOS异常检测器和双类CatBoost分类器，使用和不使用PCA压缩至64维特征向量。

Result: 双类监督模型在所有数据集上始终显著优于单类异常检测：立陶宛语最佳配置（jina）达到80.96%准确率和0.887 AUC ROC；俄语（e5）达到92.19%准确率和0.978 AUC ROC；英语（e5+PCA）达到77.21%准确率和0.859 AUC ROC。PCA压缩在监督设置中几乎保留全部判别能力，但对无监督异常检测有负面影响。

Conclusion: 现代多语言句子嵌入结合梯度提升决策树为多语言仇恨言论检测应用提供了稳健的软计算解决方案，监督分类方法优于异常检测，PCA压缩在监督场景中有效。

Abstract: Online hate speech and abusive language pose a growing challenge for content moderation, especially in multilingual settings and for low-resource languages such as Lithuanian. This paper investigates to what extent modern multilingual sentence embedding models can support accurate hate speech detection in Lithuanian, Russian, and English, and how their performance depends on downstream modeling choices and feature dimensionality. We introduce LtHate, a new Lithuanian hate speech corpus derived from news portals and social networks, and benchmark six modern multilingual encoders (potion, gemma, bge, snow, jina, e5) on LtHate, RuToxic, and EnSuperset using a unified Python pipeline. For each embedding, we train both a one class HBOS anomaly detector and a two class CatBoost classifier, with and without principal component analysis (PCA) compression to 64-dimensional feature vectors. Across all datasets, two class supervised models consistently and substantially outperform one class anomaly detection, with the best configurations achieving up to 80.96% accuracy and AUC ROC of 0.887 in Lithuanian (jina), 92.19% accuracy and AUC ROC of 0.978 in Russian (e5), and 77.21% accuracy and AUC ROC of 0.859 in English (e5 with PCA). PCA compression preserves almost all discriminative power in the supervised setting, while showing some negative impact for the unsupervised anomaly detection case. These results demonstrate how modern multilingual sentence embeddings combined with gradient boosted decision trees provide robust soft-computing solutions for multilingual hate speech detection applications.

</details>


### [126] [IE as Cache: Information Extraction Enhanced Agentic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14930)
*Hang Lv,Sheng Liang,Hongchao Gu,Wei Guo,Defu Lian,Yong Liu,Hao Wang,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 将信息抽取(IE)重构为认知缓存，通过查询驱动的抽取和缓存感知推理来增强智能体推理能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统IE仅作为终端目标，提取的结构被孤立使用而非在多步推理中维护和重用，限制了其作为认知资源的潜力

Method: 提出IE-as-Cache框架，受分层计算机内存启发，结合查询驱动的信息抽取和缓存感知推理，动态维护紧凑的中间信息并过滤噪声

Result: 在多样化LLMs的挑战性基准测试中，推理准确性显著提升，证明IE可有效重构为可重用的认知资源

Conclusion: IE可作为认知缓存增强智能体推理，为IE的下游应用研究提供了有前景的方向

Abstract: Information Extraction aims to distill structured, decision-relevant information from unstructured text, serving as a foundation for downstream understanding and reasoning. However, it is traditionally treated merely as a terminal objective: once extracted, the resulting structure is often consumed in isolation rather than maintained and reused during multi-step inference. Moving beyond this, we propose \textit{IE-as-Cache}, a framework that repurposes IE as a cognitive cache to enhance agentic reasoning. Drawing inspiration from hierarchical computer memory, our approach combines query-driven extraction with cache-aware reasoning to dynamically maintain compact intermediate information and filter noise. Experiments on challenging benchmarks across diverse LLMs demonstrate significant improvements in reasoning accuracy, indicating that IE can be effectively repurposed as a reusable cognitive resource and offering a promising direction for future research on downstream uses of IE.

</details>


### [127] [XQ-MEval: A Dataset with Cross-lingual Parallel Quality for Benchmarking Translation Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14934)
*Jingxuan Liu,Zhi Qu,Jin Tei,Hidetaka Kamigaito,Lemao Liu,Taro Watanabe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: XQ-MEval是一个半自动构建的数据集，用于评估多语言翻译指标，通过注入可控质量的错误来创建平行质量实例，揭示了跨语言评分偏差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多语言翻译系统评估通常采用跨语言平均指标分数的方法，但这种方法存在可疑之处，因为指标可能存在跨语言评分偏差——同等质量的翻译在不同语言中可能获得不同分数。由于缺乏提供跨语言平行质量实例的基准数据集，且专家标注不现实，这一问题尚未得到系统研究。

Method: 提出XQ-MEval数据集构建方法：1）将MQM定义的错误自动注入黄金翻译中；2）由母语者过滤以确保可靠性；3）合并错误生成具有可控质量的伪翻译；4）将伪翻译与对应的源文本和参考翻译配对形成三元组，用于评估翻译指标质量。覆盖9个翻译方向。

Result: 使用XQ-MEval对9个代表性指标进行实验，揭示了平均分数与人类判断之间的不一致性，首次提供了跨语言评分偏差的经验证据。提出的归一化策略能够对齐跨语言的分数分布，提高了多语言指标评估的公平性和可靠性。

Conclusion: XQ-MEval为系统研究跨语言评分偏差提供了首个基准数据集，揭示了当前多语言翻译评估方法的局限性，提出的归一化策略能够改善评估的公平性，为构建更可靠的多语言翻译系统评估框架奠定了基础。

Abstract: Automatic evaluation metrics are essential for building multilingual translation systems. The common practice of evaluating these systems is averaging metric scores across languages, yet this is suspicious since metrics may suffer from cross-lingual scoring bias, where translations of equal quality receive different scores across languages. This problem has not been systematically studied because no benchmark exists that provides parallel-quality instances across languages, and expert annotation is not realistic. In this work, we propose XQ-MEval, a semi-automatically built dataset covering nine translation directions, to benchmark translation metrics. Specifically, we inject MQM-defined errors into gold translations automatically, filter them by native speakers for reliability, and merge errors to generate pseudo translations with controllable quality. These pseudo translations are then paired with corresponding sources and references to form triplets used in assessing the qualities of translation metrics. Using XQ-MEval, our experiments on nine representative metrics reveal the inconsistency between averaging and human judgment and provide the first empirical evidence of cross-lingual scoring bias. Finally, we propose a normalization strategy derived from XQ-MEval that aligns score distributions across languages, improving the fairness and reliability of multilingual metric evaluation.

</details>


### [128] [Text2Arch: A Dataset for Generating Scientific Architecture Diagrams from Natural Language Descriptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14941)
*Shivank Garg,Sankalp Mittal,Manish Gupta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一个自动从文本生成科学架构图的系统，通过创建新数据集和微调语言模型实现，性能优于现有基线并与GPT-4o相当


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯文本描述复杂系统设计或科学过程效率低且易产生歧义，需要自动从文本生成高语义保真度的科学架构图系统

Method: 创建包含科学架构图像、文本描述和DOT代码表示的综合数据集，基于此微调小型语言模型，并与GPT-4o进行上下文学习对比

Result: 提出的模型显著优于DiagramAgent等基线模型，性能与基于上下文学习的GPT-4o生成结果相当

Conclusion: 通过创建数据集和微调语言模型，成功实现了从文本自动生成高保真度科学架构图的系统，为相关应用提供了有效解决方案

Abstract: Communicating complex system designs or scientific processes through text alone is inefficient and prone to ambiguity. A system that automatically generates scientific architecture diagrams from text with high semantic fidelity can be useful in multiple applications like enterprise architecture visualization, AI-driven software design, and educational content creation. Hence, in this paper, we focus on leveraging language models to perform semantic understanding of the input text description to generate intermediate code that can be processed to generate high-fidelity architecture diagrams. Unfortunately, no clean large-scale open-access dataset exists, implying lack of any effective open models for this task. Hence, we contribute a comprehensive dataset, \system, comprising scientific architecture images, their corresponding textual descriptions, and associated DOT code representations. Leveraging this resource, we fine-tune a suite of small language models, and also perform in-context learning using GPT-4o. Through extensive experimentation, we show that \system{} models significantly outperform existing baseline models like DiagramAgent and perform at par with in-context learning-based generations from GPT-4o. We make the code, data and models publicly available.

</details>


### [129] [Explain the Flag: Contextualizing Hate Speech Beyond Censorship](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14970)
*Jason Liartis,Eirini Kaldeli,Lambrini Gyftokosta,Eleftherios Chelioudakis,Orfeas Menis Mastromichalakis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种结合LLM与定制词汇表的混合方法，用于检测和解释英语、法语、希腊语中的仇恨言论，通过两个互补管道生成有根据的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前仇恨言论检测系统大多侧重于审查或删除，缺乏透明度且限制言论自由，无法解释内容为何有害。需要更透明、可问责且信息丰富的解决方案。

Method: 混合方法结合LLM与三个新创建的定制词汇表。两个互补管道：1) 使用词汇表检测和消歧问题术语；2) 利用LLM作为上下文感知的群体目标内容评估器。输出融合为有根据的解释。

Result: 人类评估显示该混合方法准确，解释质量高，优于纯LLM基线方法。

Conclusion: 提出的混合方法通过结合词汇表与LLM，实现了更透明、可解释的仇恨言论检测，为内容审核提供了有根据的解释，平衡了检测准确性与透明度需求。

Abstract: Hate, derogatory, and offensive speech remains a persistent challenge in online platforms and public discourse. While automated detection systems are widely used, most focus on censorship or removal, raising concerns for transparency and freedom of expression, and limiting opportunities to explain why content is harmful. To address these issues, explanatory approaches have emerged as a promising solution, aiming to make hate speech detection more transparent, accountable, and informative. In this paper, we present a hybrid approach that combines Large Language Models (LLMs) with three newly created and curated vocabularies to detect and explain hate speech in English, French, and Greek. Our system captures both inherently derogatory expressions tied to identity characteristics and direct group-targeted content through two complementary pipelines: one that detects and disambiguates problematic terms using the curated vocabularies, and one that leverages LLMs as context-aware evaluators of group-targeting content. The outputs are fused into grounded explanations that clarify why content is flagged. Human evaluation shows that our hybrid approach is accurate, with high-quality explanations, outperforming LLM-only baselines.

</details>


### [130] [IUQ: Interrogative Uncertainty Quantification for Long-Form Large Language Model Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15109)
*Haozhi Fan,Jinhao Duan,Kaidi Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: IUQ是一种用于量化长文本LLM生成不确定性的新框架，通过样本间一致性和样本内忠实性来评估不确定性，在多个模型家族和规模上表现优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLMs快速发展，但长文本生成中的不确定性量化仍是一个挑战。现有方法通常限制LLMs生成简短或受限的答案集，而许多实际应用需要长文本和自由文本生成。主要困难在于LLMs经常生成语义连贯但事实不准确的响应，同时底层语义是多方面的，语言结构复杂。

Method: 提出Interrogative Uncertainty Quantification (IUQ)框架，利用"询问-回答"范式，通过样本间一致性和样本内忠实性来量化长文本LLM输出的不确定性。该方法提供可靠的主张级不确定性度量和模型的忠实性评估。

Result: 在多个模型家族和不同模型规模上的实验结果表明，IUQ在两个广泛使用的长文本生成数据集上表现优于现有方法。代码已开源。

Conclusion: IUQ框架有效解决了长文本LLM生成中的不确定性量化问题，通过结合样本间一致性和样本内忠实性，为长文本生成提供了可靠的不确定性评估方法。

Abstract: Despite the rapid advancement of Large Language Models (LLMs), uncertainty quantification in LLM generation is a persistent challenge. Although recent approaches have achieved strong performance by restricting LLMs to produce short or constrained answer sets, many real-world applications require long-form and free-form text generation. A key difficulty in this setting is that LLMs often produce responses that are semantically coherent yet factually inaccurate, while the underlying semantics are multifaceted and the linguistic structure is complex. To tackle this challenge, this paper introduces Interrogative Uncertainty Quantification (IUQ), a novel framework that leverages inter-sample consistency and intra-sample faithfulness to quantify the uncertainty in long-form LLM outputs. By utilizing an interrogate-then-respond paradigm, our method provides reliable measures of claim-level uncertainty and the model's faithfulness. Experimental results across diverse model families and model sizes demonstrate the superior performance of IUQ over two widely used long-form generation datasets. The code is available at https://github.com/louisfanhz/IUQ.

</details>


### [131] [DiscoTrace: Representing and Comparing Answering Strategies of Humans and LLMs in Information-Seeking Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15140)
*Neha Srikanth,Jordan Boyd-Graber,Rachel Rudinger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DiscoTrace是一种识别回答者在回应信息寻求问题时使用的修辞策略的方法，通过将回答表示为问题相关话语行为序列，结合对原始问题的解释，并在修辞结构理论解析上进行标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是理解不同人类社区在回答信息寻求问题时采用的修辞策略多样性，并比较大型语言模型（LLMs）与人类回答者在修辞策略选择上的差异。

Method: 开发DiscoTrace方法，将回答表示为问题相关话语行为序列，结合问题解释，基于修辞结构理论进行标注。应用于来自九个不同人类社区的答案，并分析LLMs的回答模式。

Result: 人类社区在答案构建上表现出多样化的偏好，而LLMs即使在被提示模仿特定人类社区回答指南时，也不展示修辞多样性。LLMs系统性地选择广度，处理人类回答者选择不处理的问题解释。

Conclusion: 研究结果可以指导开发实用的LLM回答者，使其能够考虑由上下文信息在问答中提供的多种策略。

Abstract: We introduce DiscoTrace, a method to identify the rhetorical strategies that answerers use when responding to information-seeking questions. DiscoTrace represents answers as a sequence of question-related discourse acts paired with interpretations of the original question, annotated on top of rhetorical structure theory parses. Applying DiscoTrace to answers from nine different human communities reveals that communities have diverse preferences for answer construction. In contrast, LLMs do not exhibit rhetorical diversity in their answers, even when prompted to mimic specific human community answering guidelines. LLMs also systematically opt for breadth, addressing interpretations of questions that human answerers choose not to address. Our findings can guide the development of pragmatic LLM answerers that consider a range of strategies informed by context in QA.

</details>


### [132] [Compressing Sequences in the Latent Embedding Space: $K$-Token Merging for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15153)
*Zihao Xu,John Harvill,Ziwei Fan,Yizhou Sun,Hao Ding,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: K-Token Merging：一种在潜在嵌入空间压缩长提示的框架，通过将K个连续token嵌入合并为单个嵌入，实现最高75%输入长度减少且性能损失最小


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型处理长提示时计算和内存成本高，因为自注意力机制随输入长度呈二次方增长。现有提示压缩方法主要在token空间操作，忽略了潜在嵌入空间的低效性

Method: 提出K-Token Merging框架：1) 通过轻量级编码器将每个连续K个token嵌入块合并为单个嵌入；2) 压缩序列由LoRA适配的LLM处理；3) 生成仍使用原始词汇表

Result: 在结构推理(Textualized Tree)、情感分类(Amazon Reviews)和代码编辑(CommitPackFT)任务上，K-Token Merging在性能与压缩比之间达到Pareto前沿，实现最高75%输入长度减少且性能下降最小

Conclusion: K-Token Merging通过在潜在嵌入空间压缩token，有效解决了LLM处理长提示的计算效率问题，在保持性能的同时显著减少输入长度

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) incur significant computational and memory costs when processing long prompts, as full self-attention scales quadratically with input length. Token compression aims to address this challenge by reducing the number of tokens representing inputs. However, existing prompt-compression approaches primarily operate in token space and overlook inefficiencies in the latent embedding space. In this paper, we propose K-Token Merging, a latent-space compression framework that merges each contiguous block of K token embeddings into a single embedding via a lightweight encoder. The compressed sequence is processed by a LoRA-adapted LLM, while generation remains in the original vocabulary. Experiments on structural reasoning (Textualized Tree), sentiment classification (Amazon Reviews), and code editing (CommitPackFT) show that K-Token Merging lies on the Pareto frontier of performance vs. compression, achieving up to 75% input length reduction with minimal performance degradation.

</details>


### [133] [Fabricator or dynamic translator?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15165)
*Lisa Vasileva,Karin Sim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文探讨了大型语言模型在机器翻译中的过生成现象，分析了其与神经机器翻译中"神经胡言"的区别，并提出了在商业环境中检测和分类这些过生成内容的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在机器翻译中表现出色，但其生成性质可能导致各种过生成现象。这些过生成不同于神经机器翻译中的"神经胡言"，包括LLM自我解释、风险性虚构和适当解释等多种形式。准确检测和确定过生成的具体性质是一个具有挑战性的任务，特别是在商业应用环境中。

Method: 论文详细探讨了在商业环境中研究过的不同策略，包括检测和分类大型语言模型在翻译中产生的过生成内容的方法。这些策略旨在区分不同类型的过生成，如LLM自我解释、风险性虚构和适当的解释性翻译。

Result: 论文展示了在商业环境中应用不同策略检测和分类LLM翻译过生成现象的结果，但具体结果细节需要查阅完整论文才能了解。

Conclusion: 大型语言模型在机器翻译中能够像人类翻译者一样提供解释性翻译，有助于目标受众更好地理解内容，但需要有效的策略来检测和管理其过生成现象，特别是在商业应用中需要平衡翻译质量和风险控制。

Abstract: LLMs are proving to be adept at machine translation although due to their generative nature they may at times overgenerate in various ways. These overgenerations are different from the neurobabble seen in NMT and range from LLM self-explanations, to risky confabulations, to appropriate explanations, where the LLM is able to act as a human translator would, enabling greater comprehension for the target audience. Detecting and determining the exact nature of the overgenerations is a challenging task. We detail different strategies we have explored for our work in a commercial setting, and present our results.

</details>


### [134] [MADE: A Living Benchmark for Multi-Label Text Classification with Uncertainty Quantification of Medical Device Adverse Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15203)
*Raunak Agarwal,Markus Wenzel,Simon Baur,Jonas Zimmer,George Harvey,Jackie Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MADE是一个基于医疗设备不良事件报告的多标签文本分类基准，具有长尾标签分布和严格时间分割，用于评估模型性能和不确定性量化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险领域（如医疗）需要可靠的预测性能和不确定性量化。现有多标签文本分类基准存在数据污染和饱和问题，难以区分模型是真正推理还是记忆训练数据。

Method: 创建MADE基准，基于医疗设备不良事件报告，具有层次化标签的长尾分布和严格时间分割。评估20多个编码器和解码器模型，在微调和少样本设置下，系统评估基于熵/一致性和自我表达的不确定性量化方法。

Result: 发现明显权衡：小型判别式微调解码器在头尾准确率上表现最强且保持竞争力的不确定性量化；生成式微调提供最可靠的不确定性量化；大型推理模型在罕见标签上表现更好但不确定性量化较弱；自我表达的置信度不能可靠替代不确定性。

Conclusion: MADE基准为多标签文本分类提供了可靠的评估平台，揭示了不同模型在预测性能和不确定性量化之间的权衡，为高风险领域应用提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Machine learning in high-stakes domains such as healthcare requires not only strong predictive performance but also reliable uncertainty quantification (UQ) to support human oversight. Multi-label text classification (MLTC) is a central task in this domain, yet remains challenging due to label imbalances, dependencies, and combinatorial complexity. Existing MLTC benchmarks are increasingly saturated and may be affected by training data contamination, making it difficult to distinguish genuine reasoning capabilities from memorization. We introduce MADE, a living MLTC benchmark derived from {m}edical device {ad}verse {e}vent reports and continuously updated with newly published reports to prevent contamination. MADE features a long-tailed distribution of hierarchical labels and enables reproducible evaluation with strict temporal splits. We establish baselines across more than 20 encoder- and decoder-only models under fine-tuning and few-shot settings (instruction-tuned/reasoning variants, local/API-accessible). We systematically assess entropy-/consistency-based and self-verbalized UQ methods. Results show clear trade-offs: smaller discriminatively fine-tuned decoders achieve the strongest head-to-tail accuracy while maintaining competitive UQ; generative fine-tuning delivers the most reliable UQ; large reasoning models improve performance on rare labels yet exhibit surprisingly weak UQ; and self-verbalized confidence is not a reliable proxy for uncertainty. Our work is publicly available at https://hhi.fraunhofer.de/aml-demonstrator/made-benchmark.

</details>


### [135] [From Tokens to Steps: Verification-Aware Speculative Decoding for Efficient Multi-Step Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15244)
*Kiran Purohit,Ramasuri Narayanam,Soumyabrata Pal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SpecGuard：一种验证感知的推测解码框架，通过模型内部信号进行步骤级验证，提高准确率同时降低延迟


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推测解码的token中心特性会导致错误传播，现有方法使用外部奖励模型但带来额外延迟、计算开销并限制泛化能力，需要一种仅使用模型内部信号的验证方法

Method: 提出SpecGuard框架，在每个步骤采样多个草稿候选，选择最一致的步骤，然后使用两种轻量级模型内部信号进行验证：(1)基于注意力的基础评分，衡量对输入和已接受步骤的归因；(2)基于对数概率的评分，捕捉token级置信度

Result: 在多个推理基准测试中，SpecGuard相比传统推测解码和奖励引导的推测解码，准确率提高3.6%，延迟降低约11%

Conclusion: SpecGuard通过仅使用模型内部信号进行步骤级验证，有效解决了推测解码中的错误传播问题，在提高准确率的同时降低了延迟，优于现有方法

Abstract: Speculative decoding (SD) accelerates large language model inference by allowing a lightweight draft model to propose outputs that a stronger target model verifies. However, its token-centric nature allows erroneous steps to propagate. Prior approaches mitigate this using external reward models, but incur additional latency, computational overhead, and limit generalizability. We propose SpecGuard, a verification-aware speculative decoding framework that performs step-level verification using only model-internal signals. At each step, SpecGuard samples multiple draft candidates and selects the most consistent step, which is then validated using an ensemble of two lightweight model-internal signals: (i) an attention-based grounding score that measures attribution to the input and previously accepted steps, and (ii) a log-probability-based score that captures token-level confidence. These signals jointly determine whether a step is accepted or recomputed using the target, allocating compute selectively. Experiments across a range of reasoning benchmarks show that SpecGuard improves accuracy by 3.6% while reducing latency by ~11%, outperforming both SD and reward-guided SD.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [136] [Fun-TSG: A Function-Driven Multivariate Time Series Generator with Variable-Level Anomaly Labeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14221)
*Pierre Lotte,André Péninou,Olivier Teste*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Fun-TSG是一个完全可定制的时间序列生成器，用于支持异常检测系统的高质量评估，提供自动化生成和手动配置两种模式，确保数据生成过程的完全透明性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多元时间序列异常检测评估存在挑战，主要由于现有基准数据集存在局限性：缺乏细粒度异常标注、不提供明确的变量间和时间依赖关系、对底层生成机制洞察不足，这阻碍了检测模型的开发和严格比较。

Method: 引入Fun-TSG时间序列生成器，支持两种生成模式：1）基于随机采样的依赖结构和异常类型的全自动生成；2）通过用户定义方程和异常配置的手动生成。两种模式都提供数据生成过程的完全透明性。

Result: Fun-TSG能够创建多样化、可解释且可复现的基准测试场景，支持变量和时间戳级别的真实异常标签访问，为经典和现代异常检测模型提供细粒度性能分析能力。

Conclusion: Fun-TSG填补了多元时间序列异常检测评估的空白，通过提供完全可定制、透明且可复现的数据生成工具，支持更高质量的异常检测系统评估和比较。

Abstract: Reliable evaluation of anomaly detection methods in multivariate time series remains an open challenge, largely due to the limitations of existing benchmark datasets. Current resources often lack fine-grained anomaly annotations, do not provide explicit intervariable and temporal dependencies, and offer little insight into the underlying generative mechanisms. These shortcomings hinder the development and rigorous comparison of detection models, especially those targeting interpretable and variable-specific outputs. To address this gap, we introduce Fun-TSG, a fully customizable time series generator designed to support high-quality evaluation of anomaly detection systems. Our tool enables both fully automated generation, based on randomly sampled dependency structures and anomaly types, and manual generation through user-defined equations and anomaly configurations. In both cases, it provides full transparency over the data generation process, including access to ground-truth anomaly labels at the variable and timestamp levels. Fun-TSG supports the creation of diverse, interpretable, and reproducible benchmarking scenarios, enabling fine-grained performance analysis for both classical and modern anomaly detection models.

</details>


### [137] [Formalizing Kantian Ethics: Formula of the Universal Law Logic (FULL)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14254)
*Taylor Olson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于康德伦理学的形式化道德推理逻辑FULL，用于构建更自主的人工道德智能体，避免依赖预先编码的人类道德直觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器伦理领域构建人工道德智能体时存在两个主要局限：1) 不考虑智能体执行动作的目的；2) 假设人类可以枚举所有道德直觉。需要一种更自主的道德推理方法。

Method: 提出FULL（Formula of the Universal Law Logic）逻辑系统，这是一种多类量化模态逻辑，形式化了康德的普遍法则公式（FUL）以及因果关系、能动性等概念。

Result: 在康德伦理学的三个案例中证明，FULL能够在没有内置道德直觉的情况下，基于充分的非规范性背景知识，评估智能体为特定目的执行的动作。

Conclusion: FULL为构建更鲁棒和自主的人工道德智能体做出了贡献，同时提供了对康德伦理学更形式化的理解。

Abstract: The field of machine ethics aims to build Artificial Moral Agents (AMAs) to better understand morality and make AI agents safer. To do so, many approaches encode human moral intuition as a set of axioms on actions e.g., do not harm, you must help others. However, this introduces (at least) two limitations for future AMAs. First, it does not consider the agent's purposes in performing the action. Second, it assumes that we humans can enumerate our moral intuition. This paper explores formalizing a moral procedure that alleviates these two limitations. We specifically consider Kantian ethics and present a multi-sorted quantified modal logic we call the Formula of the Universal Law Logic (FULL). The FULL formalizes Kant's first formulation of the categorical imperative, the Formula of the Universal Law (FUL), and concepts such as causality and agency. We demonstrate on three cases from Kantian ethics that the FULL can reason to evaluate agents' actions for certain purposes without built-in moral intuition, given that it has sufficient (non-normative) background knowledge. Therefore, the FULL is a contribution towards more robust and autonomous AMAs, and a more formal understanding of Kantian ethics.

</details>


### [138] [GFT: From Imitation to Reward Fine-Tuning with Unbiased Group Advantages and Dynamic Coefficient Rectification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14258)
*Wangjie Gan,Miao Pan,Linbo Xi,Wenqi Zhang,Jintao Chen,Jianwei Yin,Xuhong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GFT是一种统一的后训练框架，通过组优势学习和动态系数校正解决SFT的奖励稀疏性和优化不稳定问题，优于传统SFT方法并更好地与后续RL训练结合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型语言模型通常使用监督微调(SFT)和强化学习(RL)进行后训练，但有效统一高效知识注入与鲁棒泛化仍然具有挑战性。研究发现SFT可解释为具有极端稀疏隐式奖励和不稳定逆概率加权的策略梯度优化特例，导致单一路径依赖、熵崩溃和梯度爆炸。

Method: 提出组微调(GFT)框架，包含两个核心机制：1) 组优势学习：构建多样化响应组并推导归一化对比监督以缓解奖励稀疏性；2) 动态系数校正：自适应地限制逆概率权重以稳定优化，同时保持高效知识注入。

Result: 实验表明GFT持续超越基于SFT的方法，并产生能够更平滑地与后续RL训练集成的策略。

Conclusion: GFT通过解决SFT的固有局限性，提供了一个统一的后训练框架，有效平衡了知识注入效率和泛化鲁棒性，为语言模型后训练提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models are typically post-trained using supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL), yet effectively unifying efficient knowledge injection with robust generalization remains challenging. In this work, we provide a training-dynamics analysis showing that SFT can be interpreted as a special case of policy gradient optimization with an extremely sparse implicit reward and unstable inverse-probability weighting, which together lead to single-path dependency, entropy collapse, and gradient explosion. Motivated by this diagnosis, we propose Group Fine-Tuning (GFT), a unified post-training framework that addresses these intrinsic limitations through two mechanisms: Group Advantage Learning, which constructs diverse response groups and derives normalized contrastive supervision to alleviate reward sparsity, and Dynamic Coefficient Rectification, which adaptively bounds inverse-probability weights to stabilize optimization while preserving efficient knowledge injection. Experiments demonstrate that GFT consistently surpasses SFT-based methods and yields policies that integrate more smoothly with subsequent RL training.

</details>


### [139] [Seeing Through Experts Eyes A Foundational Vision Language Model Trained on Radiologists Gaze and Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14316)
*Kinhei Lee,Peiyuan Jing,Zhenxuan Zhang,Yue Yang,Tao Wang,Dominic C Marshall,Yingying Fang,Guang Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GazeX：利用放射科医生眼动数据作为行为先验的视觉语言模型，通过模拟专家视觉检查模式提升胸部X光解读的准确性和可解释性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模视觉语言模型在胸部X光解读中存在局限性，主要问题是模型输出与放射科医生推理过程存在差距。大多数系统仅优化语义信息，未能模拟专家如何视觉检查医学图像，常常忽略关键发现或偏离既定的诊断工作流程。放射科医生遵循结构化协议（如ABCDEF方法）确保系统检查所有临床相关区域，减少遗漏发现并支持可靠的诊断推理。

Method: 引入GazeX视觉语言模型，利用放射科医生的眼动追踪数据作为行为先验来建模专家诊断推理。通过将注视轨迹和注视模式纳入预训练，GazeX学习遵循放射科医生注意力的空间和时间结构，并以临床有意义的序列整合观察。使用来自五位放射科医生的超过30,000个注视关键帧的精选数据集进行训练。

Result: GazeX在放射学报告生成、疾病定位和视觉问答任务中产生更准确、可解释且与专家一致的输出。模型利用了231,835个放射学研究、780,014个问答对和1,162个带有边界框的图像-句子对。与自主报告系统不同，GazeX产生可验证的证据工件，包括检查轨迹和发现链接的定位区域，支持高效的人工验证和安全的人机协作。

Conclusion: 通过专家眼睛学习为放射学及其他领域提供了实现更可信、可解释和诊断稳健的AI系统的实用途径。GazeX的方法通过模拟专家视觉检查模式，弥合了AI输出与临床推理之间的差距，支持可验证的人机协作。

Abstract: Large scale vision language models have shown promise in automating chest Xray interpretation, yet their clinical utility remains limited by a gap between model outputs and radiologist reasoning. Most systems optimize for semantic information without emulating how experts visually examine medical images, often overlooking critical findings or diverging from established diagnostic workflows. Radiologists follow structured protocols (e.g., the ABCDEF approach) that ensure all clinically relevant regions are systematically examined, reducing missed findings and supporting reliable diagnostic reasoning. We introduce GazeX, a vision language model that leverages radiologists' eye tracking data as a behavioral prior to model expert diagnostic reasoning. By incorporating gaze trajectories and fixation patterns into pretraining, GazeX learns to follow the spatial and temporal structure of radiologist attention and integrates observations in a clinically meaningful sequence. Using a curated dataset of over 30,000 gaze key frames from five radiologists, we demonstrate that GazeX produces more accurate, interpretable, and expert consistent outputs across radiology report generation, disease grounding, and visual question answering, utilizing 231,835 radiographic studies, 780,014 question answer pairs, and 1,162 image sentence pairs with bounding boxes. Unlike autonomous reporting systems, GazeX produces verifiable evidence artifacts, including inspection trajectories and finding linked localized regions, enabling efficient human verification and safe human AI collaboration. Learning through expert eyes provides a practical route toward more trustworthy, explainable, and diagnostically robust AI systems for radiology and beyond.

</details>


### [140] [Equifinality in Mixture of Experts: Routing Topology Does Not Determine Language Modeling Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14419)
*Ivan Ternovtsii,Yurii Bilak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现路由拓扑结构不影响语言建模的渐近困惑度，多种路由变体在统计上等效，真正的机制优势仅约1.2%


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究稀疏混合专家架构中路由拓扑是否真正决定语言建模质量，挑战当前对复杂路由机制的过度关注

Method: 构建几何MoE（ST-MoE），使用64维低维空间中余弦相似度路由，进行62个对照实验（WikiText-103，76-84M参数），采用双单侧检验统计方法

Result: 五种余弦路由变体在1-PPL范围内统计等效；路由拓扑不影响渐近困惑度；收敛冗余机制使多跳更新共线；零样本相对范数停止可节省25% MoE FLOPs

Conclusion: 路由拓扑不是决定语言建模质量的关键因素，真正的机制优势很小（~1.2%）；存在拓扑层面的等效性，但专家级专业化和因果可控性仍需关注

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures employ increasingly sophisticated routing mechanisms -- learned routers, multi-hop trajectories, token-dependent gating. We ask: does routing topology actually determine language modeling quality? We build a geometric MoE (ST-MoE) using cosine-similarity routing against learned centroids in a low-dimensional space ($d_{space} = 64$), requiring 80% fewer routing parameters than standard linear routers. Through 62 controlled experiments on WikiText-103 at 76--84M parameters trained to convergence (50K steps, 1.64B tokens), we find that routing topology does not determine asymptotic perplexity (PPL): five cosine-routing variants are statistically equivalent within a 1-PPL margin (Two One-Sided Tests [TOST], $p < 0.05$ for all 10 pairwise comparisons; 15 runs across 3 seeds, observed range 33.93--34.72). The finding extends to hash, random-fixed, and top-1 routing (single-seed; graceful 1.1--2.2 PPL degradation) and replicates on OpenWebText (0.03 PPL gap, 6 runs, 3 seeds each). A standard linear router with 5.3$\times$ more routing parameters reaches PPL 32.76, but iso-parameter cosine routing closes 67% of this gap -- the true mechanism advantage is $\sim$1.2%. The mechanistic explanation is convergent redundancy: multi-hop updates are collinear ($\cos(Δh_0, Δh_1) = 0.805$), implementing magnitude amplification rather than compositional reasoning; a single learnable scalar replicates multi-hop performance. As a practical payoff, zero-shot relative-norm halting saves 25% of MoE FLOPs at +0.12% PPL. Expert-level specialization and causal controllability -- which coexist with topology-level equifinality -- are explored in a companion paper.

</details>


### [141] [Demonstration of Pneuma-Seeker: Agentic System for Reifying and Fulfilling Information Needs on Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14422)
*Muhammad Imam Luthfi Balaka,Raul Castro Fernandez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Pneuma-Seeker系统将用户信息需求转化为可检查的关系规范，支持迭代式数据探索和溯源感知执行，利用LLM作为透明交互分析协作者而非黑盒答案引擎。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据分析师在处理关系数据时通常从模糊或不明确的问题开始，需要迭代式探索和细化。现有工具缺乏对信息需求进行明确、可检查的规范表示，难以支持迭代式数据发现和溯源跟踪。

Method: 开发Pneuma-Seeker系统，将用户信息需求具体化为明确、可检查的关系规范。系统支持信息需求的迭代细化、目标数据发现和溯源感知执行，利用大型语言模型作为透明、交互式的分析协作者。

Result: 通过两个真实世界的采购用例展示了Pneuma-Seeker如何有效支持数据分析师的迭代探索过程。系统能够将模糊信息需求转化为可操作的关系规范，并利用LLM实现透明协作而非黑盒答案生成。

Conclusion: Pneuma-Seeker通过将信息需求具体化为可检查的关系规范，为数据分析师提供了更好的迭代探索支持。该方法将LLM定位为透明交互协作者而非黑盒答案引擎，为关系数据分析工具设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Data analysts working with relational data often start with vague or underspecified questions and refine them iteratively as they explore the data. To support this iterative process, we demonstrate Pneuma-Seeker, a system that reifies a user's information need as explicit, inspectable relational specifications, enabling iterative refinement of the information need, targeted data discovery, and provenance-aware execution. Through two real-world procurement use cases, we show how Pneuma-Seeker leverages LLMs as transparent, interactive analytical collaborators rather than opaque answer engines.

</details>


### [142] [Geometric Routing Enables Causal Expert Control in Mixture of Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14434)
*Ivan Ternovtsii,Yurii Bilak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 稀疏混合专家模型中的专家具有可解释的单语义特性，通过余弦相似度路由在低维度量空间中实现专家专业化的直接检查，支持因果干预验证和推理时控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管稀疏混合专家模型通过固定每个token的活跃计算来扩展参数，但个体专家的专业化特性仍然不透明。虽然路由拓扑结构对质量是中性的，但专家身份具有因果意义，需要揭示其可解释性。

Method: 通过四个证据线分析专家专业化：1）通过解嵌入矩阵投影专家输出向量构建语义词典；2）分析路由中的频率-句法梯度；3）进行因果干预验证专家标签；4）比较不同路由方法的可解释性。

Result: 15%的专家是单语义专家，涵盖10个语义类别；路由显示早期层按词频分离token，深层按句法类别分离；因果干预证实专家标签有效性，如朝向时间专家中心增加时间概率+321%；余弦路由提供几何透明度，专家专业化可直接从中心矩阵读取。

Conclusion: MoE专家级专业化是一流的可解释性原语：架构上单语义、因果验证、推理时零开销可控。余弦路由提供几何透明度，使专家专业化可直接检查，为模型可解释性提供新途径。

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models scale parameters while fixing active computation per token, but the specialization of individual experts remains opaque. In a companion paper we showed that routing topology is quality-neutral: five structurally different configurations converge to statistically equivalent language modeling quality. Here we show that expert identity is nonetheless causally meaningful: individual rank-1 experts are monosemantic by construction, and cosine-similarity routing in a low-dimensional metric space makes their specialization directly inspectable.
  We present four lines of evidence. First, projecting expert output vectors through the unembedding matrix yields a Semantic Dictionary: 15% of experts are monosemantic specialists spanning 10 categories (temporal, geographic, cardinal, discourse, emotional, financial, military, scientific). Second, routing exhibits a frequency-to-syntax gradient: early layers separate tokens by word frequency, deeper layers by syntactic class (Zipf-confound controls, all $p < 0.001$). Third, causal interventions confirm these labels: steering toward a temporal expert's centroid increases P(temporal) by +321% (median across 44 prompts); suppressing a geographic expert drops P(geographic) by -23%; rewriting an expert's output vector halves target-category probability, and effects compose additively across layers. Fourth, the interventions are not unique to cosine routing: linear routers support comparable steering, but only cosine routing provides geometric transparency -- expert specialization is readable directly from the centroid matrix.
  MoE expert-level specialization is a first-class interpretability primitive: architecturally monosemantic, causally validated, and controllable at inference with zero overhead.

</details>


### [143] [On Tackling Complex Tasks with Reward Machines and Signal Temporal Logics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14440)
*Ana María Gómez Ruiz,Thao Dang,Alexandre Donzé*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于强化学习的控制设计框架，将奖励机与信号时序逻辑结合，用于处理复杂任务的事件生成和奖励表示


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要处理复杂任务的强化学习控制设计，传统方法在复杂任务表示和训练引导方面存在不足

Method: 扩展奖励机概念，结合信号时序逻辑公式进行事件生成，利用STL在线监控算法实现框架

Result: 在minigrid、cart-pole和high-way三个非平凡任务环境中验证了框架的有效性

Conclusion: 提出的框架能够更高效地表示复杂任务奖励，并引导训练过程收敛到满足指定要求的行为

Abstract: We propose a Reinforcement Learning (RL) based control design framework for handling complex tasks. The approach extends the concept of Reward Machines (RM) with Signal Temporal Logic (STL) formulas that can be used for event generation. The use of STL allows not only a more efficient representation of rewards for complex tasks but also guiding the training process to converge towards behaviors satisfying specified requirements. We also propose an implementation of the framework that leverages the STL online monitoring algorithms. We illustrate the framework with three case studies (minigrid, cart-pole and high-way environments) with non-trivial tasks.

</details>


### [144] [AIBuildAI: An AI Agent for Automatically Building AI Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14455)
*Ruiyi Zhang,Peijia Qin,Qi Cao,Li Zhang,Pengtao Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AIBuildAI：一个基于分层智能体架构的AI系统，能够从任务描述和训练数据自动构建AI模型，超越了传统AutoML的局限性，在MLE-Bench基准测试中达到63.1%的奖牌率，媲美经验丰富的AI工程师。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI模型开发仍然高度依赖专家经验，需要人工迭代设计架构、工程表示、实现训练管道和实验评估。现有AutoML方法仅能处理超参数优化和模型选择等狭窄方面，无法覆盖完整的AI开发生命周期。需要一种能够端到端自动化AI模型开发的解决方案。

Method: 提出AIBuildAI分层智能体架构：一个管理器智能体协调三个专业子智能体：1）设计器（建模策略）、2）编码器（实现和调试）、3）调优器（训练和性能优化）。每个子智能体都是基于大语言模型的智能体，具备多步推理和工具使用能力，实现从任务描述到可部署模型的端到端自动化。

Result: 在MLE-Bench基准测试（包含视觉、文本、时间序列和表格模态的真实Kaggle风格任务）上，AIBuildAI以63.1%的奖牌率排名第一，超越了所有现有基线方法，达到了经验丰富AI工程师的水平。

Conclusion: 分层智能体系统能够自动化从任务规范到可部署模型的完整AI模型开发过程，为实现最小化人工干预的广泛可访问AI开发提供了可行路径。

Abstract: AI models underpin modern intelligent systems, driving advances across science, medicine, finance, and technology. Yet developing high-performing AI models remains a labor-intensive process that requires expert practitioners to iteratively design architectures, engineer representations, implement training pipelines and refine approaches through empirical evaluation. Existing AutoML methods partially alleviate this burden but remain limited to narrow aspects such as hyperparameter optimization and model selection within predefined search spaces, leaving the full development lifecycle largely dependent on human expertise. To address this gap, we introduce AIBuildAI, an AI agent that automatically builds AI models from a task description and training data. AIBuildAI adopts a hierarchical agent architecture in which a manager agent coordinates three specialized sub-agents: a designer for modeling strategy, a coder for implementation and debugging, and a tuner for training and performance optimization. Each sub-agent is itself a large language model (LLM) based agent capable of multi-step reasoning and tool use, enabling end-to-end automation of the AI model development process that goes beyond the scope of existing AutoML approaches. We evaluate AIBuildAI on MLE-Bench, a benchmark of realistic Kaggle-style AI development tasks spanning visual, textual, time-series and tabular modalities. AIBuildAI ranks first on MLE-Bench with a medal rate of 63.1%, outperforming all existing baseline methods and matching the capability of highly experienced AI engineers. These results demonstrate that hierarchical agent systems can automate the full AI model development process from task specification to deployable model, suggesting a pathway toward broadly accessible AI development with minimal human intervention.

</details>


### [145] [Improving Human Performance with Value-Aware Interventions: A Case Study in Chess](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14465)
*Saumik Narayanan,Raja Panjwani,Siddhartha Sen,Chien-Ju Ho*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于价值感知的AI干预方法，通过检测策略-价值不一致性来识别干预时机，在象棋实验中优于传统最优动作推荐方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统在顺序决策任务中辅助人类时，如何确定干预时机和方式是关键挑战。传统方法推荐最优动作，但假设人类能完美执行后续动作，而实际上人类决策者可能执行不佳，反而降低整体性能。

Method: 基于强化学习中的贝尔曼方程原理，提出价值感知干预方法。当决策者遵循次优策略时，策略-价值一致性不再成立，这种不一致性自然识别出干预机会。在马尔可夫决策过程中形式化该问题，AI助手可在干预预算内覆盖人类动作。对于单次干预，最优策略是推荐最大化人类价值函数的动作；对于多次干预，提出基于策略-价值差异幅度的可处理近似方法。

Result: 在象棋领域通过大规模游戏数据学习人类模型进行验证。模拟实验中，该方法在广泛设置下持续优于基于最强象棋引擎（Stockfish）的干预。在20名玩家和600场游戏的受试者研究中，该方法显著提高了低技能和中技能玩家的表现，同时在高技能玩家中与专家引擎干预效果相当。

Conclusion: 价值感知干预方法通过检测策略-价值不一致性来识别干预时机，比传统最优动作推荐更有效，特别是在人类可能无法完美执行后续动作的情况下。该方法在象棋实验中表现出优越性，为AI辅助人类顺序决策提供了新思路。

Abstract: AI systems are increasingly used to assist humans in sequential decision-making tasks, yet determining when and how an AI assistant should intervene remains a fundamental challenge. A potential baseline is to recommend the optimal action according to a strong model. However, such actions assume optimal follow-up actions, which human decision makers may fail to execute, potentially reducing overall performance. In this work, we propose and study value-aware interventions, motivated by a basic principle in reinforcement learning: under the Bellman equation, the optimal policy selects actions that maximize the immediate reward plus the value function. When a decision maker follows a suboptimal policy, this policy-value consistency no longer holds, creating discrepancies between the actions taken by the policy and those that maximize the immediate reward plus the value of the next state. We show that these policy-value inconsistencies naturally identify opportunities for intervention. We formalize this problem in a Markov decision process where an AI assistant may override human actions under an intervention budget. In the single-intervention regime, we show that the optimal strategy is to recommend the action that maximizes the human value function. For settings with multiple interventions, we propose a tractable approximation that prioritizes interventions based on the magnitude of the policy-value discrepancy. We evaluate these ideas in the domain of chess by learning models of humans from large-scale gameplay data. In simulation, our approach consistently outperforms interventions based on the strongest chess engine (Stockfish) in a wide range of settings. A within-subject human study with 20 players and 600 games further shows that our interventions significantly improve performance for low- and mid-skill players while matching expert-engine interventions for high-skill players.

</details>


### [146] [Response-Aware User Memory Selection for LLM Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14473)
*Jillian Fisher,Jennifer Neville,Chan Young Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RUMS是一种基于互信息优化的记忆选择方法，通过测量记忆子集与模型输出之间的互信息来选择用户记忆项，相比基于语义相似性的方法能更有效地减少响应不确定性并提升预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大型语言模型个性化方法主要基于用户记忆项与输入查询之间的语义相似性来选择记忆子集，忽略了这些特征如何实际影响模型的响应分布。这导致记忆选择不够优化，无法最大化模型响应的质量。

Method: 提出响应效用优化的记忆选择方法（RUMS），通过测量记忆子集与模型输出之间的互信息来选择用户记忆项。该方法识别那些能够减少响应不确定性、锐化预测的记忆项，超越了单纯的语义相似性匹配。

Result: RUMS实现了比现有最先进方法更符合人类选择的记忆选择，甚至优于400倍大的模型。使用RUMS选择的记忆项能带来更好的响应质量，同时计算成本降低高达95%。

Conclusion: 基于信息论基础的RUMS方法为记忆选择提供了更原则性的框架，能够更有效地选择对模型响应有实际影响的记忆项，在提升响应质量的同时显著降低计算成本。

Abstract: A common approach to personalization in large language models (LLMs) is to incorporate a subset of the user memory into the prompt at inference time to guide the model's generation. Existing methods select these subsets primarily using similarity between user memory items and input queries, ignoring how features actually affect the model's response distribution. We propose Response-Utility optimization for Memory Selection (RUMS), a novel method that selects user memory items by measuring the mutual information between a subset of memory and the model's outputs, identifying items that reduce response uncertainty and sharpen predictions beyond semantic similarity. We demonstrate that this information-theoretic foundation enables more principled user memory selection that aligns more closely with human selection compared to state-of-the-art methods, and models $400\times$ larger. Additionally, we show that memory items selected using RUMS result in better response quality compared to existing approaches, while having up to $95\%$ reduction in computational cost.

</details>


### [147] [Pushing the Limits of On-Device Streaming ASR: A Compact, High-Accuracy English Model for Low-Latency Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14493)
*Nenad Banfic,David Fan,Kunal Vaishnavi,Sam Kemp,Sunghoon Choi,Rui Ren,Sayan Shaw,Meng Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了在边缘设备CPU上部署高质量ASR的优化方案，通过对比多种架构和量化策略，最终实现了在保持准确率的同时大幅减小模型体积的流式ASR系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在边缘设备上部署高质量自动语音识别面临挑战：需要同时优化准确率、延迟和内存占用，且只能在CPU上运行而无GPU加速。现有方案在资源受限硬件上的实时流式ASR性能不足。

Method: 1. 系统实证研究：评估超过50种ASR架构配置（编码器-解码器、转换器、LLM基），涵盖批量、分块和流式推理模式；2. 选择NVIDIA Nemotron Speech Streaming作为基础架构；3. 在ONNX Runtime中重新实现完整流式推理管道；4. 评估多种后训练量化策略（重要性加权k-quant、混合精度、最近舍入量化）结合图级算子融合。

Result: 1. 模型从2.47 GB压缩至最小0.67 GB，同时保持词错误率在完整精度PyTorch基线的1%绝对误差内；2. 推荐的int4 k-quant变体在8个标准基准测试中实现8.20%的平均流式WER；3. 在CPU上以0.56秒算法延迟舒适地快于实时处理，为设备端流式ASR建立了新的质量-效率帕累托最优点。

Conclusion: 通过系统架构评估和量化优化，成功实现了在资源受限边缘设备CPU上部署高质量实时流式ASR的解决方案，为设备端ASR部署提供了实用的质量-效率平衡方案。

Abstract: Deploying high-quality automatic speech recognition (ASR) on edge devices requires models that jointly optimize accuracy, latency, and memory footprint while operating entirely on CPU without GPU acceleration. We conduct a systematic empirical study of state-of-the-art ASR architectures, encompassing encoder-decoder, transducer, and LLM-based paradigms, evaluated across batch, chunked, and streaming inference modes. Through a comprehensive benchmark of over 50 configurations spanning OpenAI Whisper, NVIDIA Nemotron, Parakeet TDT, Canary, Conformer Transducer, and Qwen3-ASR, we identify NVIDIA's Nemotron Speech Streaming as the strongest candidate for real-time English streaming on resource-constrained hardware. We then re-implement the complete streaming inference pipeline in ONNX Runtime and conduct a controlled evaluation of multiple post-training quantization strategies, including importance-weighted k-quant, mixed-precision schemes, and round-to-nearest quantization, combined with graph-level operator fusion. These optimizations reduce the model from 2.47 GB to as little as 0.67 GB while maintaining word error rate (WER) within 1% absolute of the full-precision PyTorch baseline. Our recommended configuration, the int4 k-quant variant, achieves 8.20% average streaming WER across eight standard benchmarks, running comfortably faster than real-time on CPU with 0.56 s algorithmic latency, establishing a new quality-efficiency Pareto point for on-device streaming ASR.

</details>


### [148] [Improving Machine Learning Performance with Synthetic Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14498)
*Mel Sohm,Charles Dezons,Sami Sellami,Oscar Ninou,Axel Pincon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文形式化分析了金融机器学习中合成数据增强的统计效应，揭示了其引发的结构偏差-方差权衡，并提出了分离信息增益与样本量效应的检验方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据增强在金融机器学习中被广泛用于缓解数据稀缺问题，但其统计作用机制尚未得到充分理解。研究者需要明确合成数据如何影响模型性能，以及何时能真正带来改进而非引入偏差。

Method: 1. 将合成数据增强形式化为训练分布的有效修改；2. 引入大小匹配的零增强作为对照，分离信息增益与机械样本量效应；3. 提出有限样本、非参数块置换检验方法，适用于弱时间依赖场景；4. 在受控马尔可夫切换环境和真实金融数据集上进行评估。

Result: 1. 合成数据增强仅在方差主导的机制中有效（如持续性波动率预测）；2. 在偏差主导的设置中会损害性能（如接近有效的方向预测）；3. 罕见机制定向增强可改善特定领域指标，但可能与无条件置换推断冲突；4. 评估了多种生成器（自举法、基于copula的模型、VAE、扩散模型、TimeGAN）在不同条件下的表现。

Conclusion: 合成数据增强在金融学习中存在结构性的偏差-方差权衡：当方差主导时能改善性能，当偏差主导时反而会损害性能。研究提供了判断合成数据何时能真正提升金融学习性能的理论框架，并揭示了其可能引发的持续性分布扭曲风险。

Abstract: Synthetic augmentation is increasingly used to mitigate data scarcity in financial machine learning, yet its statistical role remains poorly understood. We formalize synthetic augmentation as a modification of the effective training distribution and show that it induces a structural bias--variance trade-off: while additional samples may reduce estimation error, they may also shift the population objective whenever the synthetic distribution deviates from regions relevant under evaluation. To isolate informational gains from mechanical sample-size effects, we introduce a size-matched null augmentation and a finite-sample, non-parametric block permutation test that remains valid under weak temporal dependence.
  We evaluate this framework in both controlled Markov-switching environments and real financial datasets, including high-frequency option trade data and a daily equity panel. Across generators spanning bootstrap, copula-based models, variational autoencoders, diffusion models, and TimeGAN, we vary augmentation ratio, model capacity, task type, regime rarity, and signal-to-noise. We show that synthetic augmentation is beneficial only in variance-dominant regimes, such as persistent volatility forecasting-while it deteriorates performance in bias-dominant settings, including near-efficient directional prediction. Rare-regime targeting can improve domain-specific metrics but may conflict with unconditional permutation inference. Our results provide a structural perspective on when synthetic data improves financial learning performance and when it induces persistent distributional distortion.

</details>


### [149] [Geometric Metrics for MoE Specialization: From Fisher Information to Early Failure Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14500)
*Dongxin Guo,Jikun Wu,Siu Ming Yiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于信息几何的MoE专家专业化理论框架，证明传统指标缺乏参数化不变性，引入Fisher专业化指数和Fisher异质性分数两个新指标，在性能和故障预测上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE专家专业化度量指标（如余弦相似度、路由熵）缺乏理论基础，在不同参数化下结论不一致，需要建立严谨的理论框架来准确表征MoE专业化动态。

Method: 采用信息几何框架，将专家路由分布视为概率单纯形上的演化过程，使用Fisher信息度量进行黎曼几何分析，提出Fisher专业化指数(FSI)和Fisher异质性分数(FHS)两个新指标。

Result: FSI与下游性能相关性达0.91±0.02；FHS在训练完成10%时预测故障的AUC为0.89±0.03，比基于验证损失的早停方法提升23%，计算周期减少40倍；干预协议在FHS>1时恢复率达87%。

Conclusion: 信息几何框架为MoE专业化提供了首个严格理论表征，新指标在语言建模、视觉MoE和扩展研究中验证有效，显著优于传统启发式方法，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Expert specialization is fundamental to Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) model success, yet existing metrics (cosine similarity, routing entropy) lack theoretical grounding and yield inconsistent conclusions under reparameterization. We present an information-geometric framework providing the first rigorous characterization of MoE specialization dynamics. Our key insight is that expert routing distributions evolve on the probability simplex equipped with the Fisher information metric, enabling formal analysis via Riemannian geometry. We prove that standard heuristic metrics violate parameterization invariance (Theorem 1), establish that specialization corresponds to geodesic flow with quantified approximation bounds (Theorem 2), and derive a failure predictor with theoretical threshold justification (Theorem 3). The framework introduces two principled metrics: Fisher Specialization Index (FSI) achieving r=0.91+/-0.02 correlation with downstream performance, and Fisher Heterogeneity Score (FHS) predicting training failure at 10% completion with AUC=0.89+/-0.03 -- outperforming validation-loss-based early stopping by 23% while requiring 40x fewer compute cycles. We validate intervention protocols achieving 87% recovery rate when FHS>1 is detected. Comprehensive experiments across language modeling (WikiText-103, C4), vision MoE (ImageNet), and scaling studies (8-64 experts, 125M-2.7B parameters) validate our theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [150] [Perspective on Bias in Biomedical AI: Preventing Downstream Healthcare Disparities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14514)
*Michal Rosen-Zvi,Yoav Kan-Tor,Michael Danziger,Agata Ferretti,Javier Aula-Blasco,Julia Falcao,Ron Shamir,Mordechai Muszkat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文分析显示生物医学研究中存在严重的群体偏见问题：组学数据收集时人口统计学信息报告不足，欧洲血统数据在主流数据库中占主导地位，这可能导致生物医学基础模型放大早期偏见，造成医疗不平等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于揭示医疗不平等不仅源于临床实施阶段的不平等获取，更源于数据收集和研究优先级的早期偏见。当前大量组学研究缺乏人口统计学信息报告，且现有数据存在明显的群体偏向，这可能被新兴的生物医学基础模型放大，导致系统性医疗不平等。

Method: 采用自动化分析方法，对2015-2024年间4719篇PubMed索引的组学出版物进行分析，评估其人口统计学信息报告情况。同时分析常用于模型训练的大规模数据集（如CellxGene和GEO），揭示其中的群体分布偏差。

Result: 分析结果显示：1）仅有少量组学出版物报告血统或种族信息，血统报告略有改善但仍不足；2）主流数据集（CellxGene、GEO）存在显著的群体偏见，欧洲血统数据占主导地位；3）生物医学基础模型可能放大这些早期偏见，导致级联不平等。

Conclusion: 为改善生物医学AI的公平性和鲁棒性，提出社区范围内的三大基础原则：来源可追溯性、开放性和评估透明度。这一方法旨在促进更有效服务未充分服务人群的生物医学创新，改善健康结果。

Abstract: Healthcare disparities persist across socioeconomic boundaries, often attributed to unequal access to screening, diagnostics, and therapeutics. However, this perspective highlights that critical biases can emerge much earlier, during data collection and research prioritization, long before clinical implementation in cases where the focus of the studies and the data that is collected is at the molecular level. A vast number of studies focus on collecting omics data but the demographic information associated with these datasets is often not reported in the studies, and when it is reported, it shows big biases. An automated analysis of 4719 PubMed-indexed omics publications from 2015 to 2024 reveals that only a small fraction report ancestry or ethnicity information, with ancestry reporting improving slightly. Analysis of large-scale datasets commonly used for model training, such as CellxGene and GEO, reveals substantial population bias where European-ancestry data dominates. As biomedical foundation models become central to biomedical discovery with a paradigm in which base models are pretrained on large datasets and reusing them time and again for many different downstream tasks, they risk perpetuating or amplifying these early-stage biases, leading to cascading inequities that regulatory interventions cannot fully reverse. We propose a community-wide focus on three foundational principles: Provenance, Openness, and Evaluation Transparency to improve equity and robustness in biomedical AI. This approach aims to foster biomedical innovation that more effectively serves underserved populations and improves health outcomes.

</details>
